Title: Fama, y obras posthumas del fenix de Mexico, decima musa, poetisa americana, Sor Juana Ines de la Cruz, religiosa professa en el convento de San Geronimo de la imperial ciudad de Mexico
Creator: Juana Inés de la Cruz; collected and dedicated by Juan Ignacio de Castorena y Ursúa; with the approbation and life by Diego Calleja, S.J.
Publisher: Madrid: en la Imprenta de Manuel Ruiz de Murga, 1700
Publication Date: 1700
Rights: Public Domain (published 1700, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: spa
Source: Internet Archive item famayobrasposthu00juan (https://archive.org/details/famayobrasposthu00juan), the item's own famayobrasposthu00juan_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1700. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: c1fb20a02c34627e4cc7d02d34ecb96dd38bb63c6c5f25fdfdd3b98a43376d37.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. John Carter Brown Library scan; usable OCR with the long s read as f and some broken lines in the verse and in the prefatory poems. The third volume of her works, issued five years after her death.
Content note: The posthumous volume of Sor Juana's works: Calleja's approbation, which is the first life of her; her reply to 'Sor Filotea' (the bishop of Puebla), her own account of her vocation and her study; the devotional exercises for the nine days before the Incarnation and the offerings of the Rosary of the Sorrows; poems; and the eulogies of her admirers in Spain and Mexico.

---
